The optimal continuous f maybe can be found by solving an integral equation via iterative methods. Let's derive continuous KKT more carefully and see if can implement numerical BFGS in function space.

Continuum: minimize H(f)=max_{x∈[0,2]} h(x), h=(f*f), subject ∫=1, f≥0.
KKT: ∃H, p(x)≥0 supported on {h=H}, μ such that for a.e. t in support f>0:
∂/∂f(t) max h = ∫ p(x) f(x-t) dx = μ.
Also ∫f=1, h(x)=H on support p.
This is similar to Lyapunov/spectral design. We can approximate with finite elements and use nonlinear solver. But perhaps simple iterative algorithm:
1. Start f.
2. Compute h, H, p = empirical distribution on argmax (or smoothed softmax).
3. Solve for new f from operator? The KKT in f: (T_p f)(t)=μ on support, where (T_p f)(t)=∫ p(x)f(x-t)dx. This is linear? It involves f multiplied by p integrated; T_p is integral operator with kernel p(x-t). In discrete, y=p (length lags). Need find f≥0 with convolution support condition: a * y = μ (for active lags y nonzero), plus convolution f*f = H on active set. This is system. Could solve by fixed point: given y, find f by deconvolution (a_i such that a*y = μ on all i? maybe). Then compute h active, update y as weights on active h (e.g., y∝1/h? to make h flat). This resembles dual LP: y from active peaks to equalize h. Could implement.

LP dual variables y (not p) solve min sum y s.t. a*y ≥1. If f at optimum with active constraints, y>0 only active and equivalence C^T y = ρ 1 (if full support). This gives a relation. We can iteratively enforce by spectral divide:

   Compute G via cross_conv(a, p).

Detailed top-level functions:
- conv_self(a):
```
def conv_self(a):
    n=len(a)
    m=2*n-1
    mfft=1
    while mfft < m: mfft <<=1
    A=np.fft.rfft(a, mfft)
    return np.fft.irfft(A*A, mfft)[:m]
```
Maybe cache mfft? We can compute each call. For speed, use `int(math.ceil(math.log2(m)))`? Loop okay. Could create helper `pow2_ge(x)`.

- score_from_c(c,a): S=sum; return 2n*max(c)/S². For normalized sum1 just.
- score(a): c=conv_self; S=a.sum(); if S<... return inf; return 2*len(a)*max(c)/S*S.

- normalize(a, zero_eps=None): S=sum; if S<=0 return uniform; a=np.clip(a,0,1000); return a/S. For internal maybe don't clip 1000? But okay.

- interp_len(parent,n): if len parent==n return parent.copy; x_old=np.linspace(0,1,len); x_new=np.linspace(0,1,n); y=np.interp(x_new,x_old,parent); return y.

- sym(a): return normalize(0.5*(a+a[::-1])) (if symmetric maybe lose sum? normalize).

- build_inits(n, rng, parent): returns list.
   inits=[]
   if parent: inits.append(interp_len(parent,n)); inits.append(sym(interp)); maybe lowpass?

Mirror function top-level:
```
def mirror_flatten(a, deadline, rng, t_values=None, max_iters=100000, min_improve=1e-12):
   a=normalize(a)
   n=len(a); L=2n-1; M=...; Mcross=...
   best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=score(a)
   H_lower=0? maybe not.
   t = 20.0
   no_improve=0
   iter=0
   while time.monotonic() < deadline:
      c=conv_self(a)
      H=c.max(); sc=2*n*H  (sum 1)
      if sc<best_sc: best...
      if H <= 1.001/(2*n-1): break
      # weights
      z = t*(c-H)/H
      zmax=z.max(); p=np.exp(z-zmax); p=p/p.sum()
      p=0.85*p+0.15*np.full(L,1.0/L) # maybe mix
      G=grad_from_weights(a,p,n)
      g=G - G.mean()
      g_range=g.max()-g.min()
      if g_range < 1e-15: perturb
      kappas=(0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.5,1.0,2.0)
      loga=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300))
      best_cand=None; best_Cand_sc=sc+1
      for kap in kappas:
          tau=kap/g_range
          # cap log delta range 50
          delta = -tau*g
          drange=delta.max()-delta.min()
          if drange>50: delta*=50/drange
          a_cand=np.exp(loga+delta-delta.max())
          s=a_cand.sum(); if s<=0 continue
          a_cand/=s
          sc_c=score(a_cand)
          if sc_c<best_Cand_sc: ...
      if best_cand_sc < sc*(1-1e-13): accept a=best_cand; no_improve=0; t=max(5, t*1.05)
      else: no_improve++; if no_improve>20: adjust t = t*1.5 if t<200 else 5? or perturb; a = best_a*(1+0.02*normal) renorm; t=20; no_improve=0
      iter++
   return best_sc,best_a
```
Should we always use a_best for line? If current not improving, using best_a as restart. We'll set if no_improve threshold, a = best_a + noise. Also maybe sym best.

grad_from_weights:
```
def gradient_weights(a,p,n):
    L=2*n-1
    M=3*n-2; mfft=1; while mfft<M: mfft<<=1
    Pp=p[::-1].copy() # length L
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,mfft); P=np.fft.rfft(Pp,mfft)
    C=np.fft.irfft(A*P,mfft)
    idx_start=n-1; idx_end=2*n-1
    G=2.0*C[idx_start:idx_end][::-1]
    return G
```
Check length idx_end? If n=1, L=1, M=1? n positive. For n=1, no need. Ensure idx_end <= mfft. M=3n-2, mfft>=M. Slicing C up to 2n-1, which is <3n-2 for n>1. Good.

Potential cost: computing G uses mfft 3n, while score uses 2n. We can combine? For mirror, need c and G. c via separate size 2n. Could compute c from full? mfft_cross 3n can also get c as C_aA? We'd need A_self size. Keep separate.

def target_newton(a, deadline, rng, alpha_blend=0.0, score_target=1.5027, initial_drop=0.004):
   a=normalize(a); n; L; M=...
   best_sc,best_a
   H_lower = max(1.0/(2*n-1)+1e-12, 1.4/(2*n)) maybe 1.4 score? Too low. Use dynamic.
   Ht = current_H*(1-initial_drop)
   drop=initial_drop
   blend=alpha_blend (0 to 0.5)
   no_improve=0; theta_schedule=[0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.4,0.8,1.2]
   while time<deadline:
      c=conv_self(a); H=c.max(); sc=2nH; update best
      if H <= H_lower: break
      # if Ht not below H, adjust
      if Ht >= H:
          Ht = max(H_lower, H*(1-drop))
      target = build_target(c, Ht, n, blend)
      if target is None: # infeasible
          drop=max(0.0005, drop*0.5); Ht=H*(1-drop); no_improve++; if no_improve>30 break; continue
      A=np.fft.rfft(a,M); A2=A*A
      target_hat=np.fft.rfft(target,M)
      err=target_hat-A2
      # maybe scale err? ensure zero freq 0? set err[0]=0
      absA=np.abs(A); floor=1e-8*max(absA) maybe; d=np.zeros_like(A)
      mask=absA>floor; d[mask]=err[mask]/(2*A[mask])
      for th in theta_schedule:
          Anew=A+th*d
          anew=np.fft.ifft(Anew).real[:n]
          anew=np.maximum(anew,0)
          S=anew.sum(); if S<... continue
          anew/=S
          sc_c=score(a_cand)
          if best: accept
      if accept:
          no_improve=0
          # lower target: reduce Ht further based on best H?
          if Ht > best_sc/(2n): Ht = max(H_lower, 0.5*Ht +0.5*best_sc/(2n)*0.995?)
          drop=max(0.001, drop*0.8) # decrease relative drop
      else:
          no_improve++
          if no_improve>10: drop=max(0.0003, drop*0.7)
          if no_improve>40: perturb a=best_a*(1+0.01*normal); blend=...; no_improve=0
   return best
```

Build target with blend:
```
def build_target(c, Ht, n, blend):
    L=2*n-1
    t=np.minimum(c, Ht)
    deficit=1.0 - t.sum()
    if deficit < -1e-12: # shouldn't
       t=t/t.sum()
    if deficit > 0:
       mask=t < Ht*(1-1e-12)
       if not mask.any(): return None
       room=Ht - t[mask]
       room_sum=room.sum()
       if room_sum <= 1e-15: return None
       t[mask] += deficit * room / room_sum
    # slight smooth blend
    if blend > 0:
       base=base_shape(n,Ht)
       t = (1-blend)*t + blend*base
       t=np.maximum(t,0)
       S=t.sum(); if S<=0 return None; t/=S
       t=np.minimum(t,Ht)
       # if sum<1 after cap, add? redo?
       deficit=1-t.sum()
       if deficit>0:
          mask=t<Ht
          room=(Ht-t[mask]).sum()
          if room<deficit: return None
          t[mask]+=deficit*room? Need room per mask.
    # due float, normalize t to sum1? But if normalize, max may exceed Ht. Instead:
    S=t.sum();
    if S > 0:
       # scale down if S>1? Then max lower, sum not1. Need adjust to sum1 without exceeding Ht.
       if S > 1 + 1e-12:
          # lower all proportionally? That reduces sum; need add? Actually S should 1.
       if abs(S-1)>1e-10:
          # if S<1, add deficit; if S>1, reduce uniformly? But target sum must1.
    return t
```
Since t initially sum=1 and after adding sum=1. Blend with base sum1 (if base normalized) gives sum1. Capping may reduce sum; then add deficit. Implement a helper `adjust_target_to_sum_cap(t,Ht)`:
```
def fit_target_sum_cap(t,Ht):
    t=np.array(t,float)
    L=len(t)
    S=t.sum()
    if S<=0: return None
    if S > 1:
       # need reduce sum by excess without exceeding cap (cap higher). Just scale? Scaling reduces max; okay as target max lower. But target sum must1. Wait if S>1, to reduce to1, multiply by 1/S, no cap issue. But c_water t sum=1 before blend? base normalized sum1; blend sum1. Capping can only reduce sum, so S<1. So ignore >1.
    while S+1e-12 <1:
       deficit=1-S
       mask=t < Ht - 1e-15
       if not any: return None
       room=Ht - t[mask]
       room_sum=room.sum()
       if room_sum <= 1e-15: return None
       step = min(1.0, deficit / room_sum) # actually adding step*room gives deficit*step; to reach deficit use step=deficit/room_sum <=1 if feasible
       t[mask] += step*room
       S=t.sum()
       if S > 1 - 1e-15: break
    t = t/t.sum() # but scaling might exceed Ht? If S slightly <1, scale up may push cap. Better after adding exactly; but normalize to fix tiny. If t.sum()<1, and some at cap, scaling uniform might exceed. Use if S<1-1e-12 return None else t/=S.
    return t
```
But if deficit/room_sum >1 due numerical infeasible, return None.

Base shape `base_shape(n,Ht)`:
   L=2n-1
   x=np.linspace(0,2,L)
   # H_cont=Ht*n; if n=1? 
   Hc=Ht*float(n)
   if Hc <= 0.5: Hc=0.5

```
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize

DOCSTRING

def pow2_ge(m):
    m=int(m)
    if m<=1: return 1
    return 1 << (m-1).bit_length()

def clip_nonneg(a): return np.clip(np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64),0,1000)

def normalize_seq(a, floor=0.0):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a=np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    a=np.clip(a,0,1000)
    s=float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15:
       return np.ones(len(a))/max(1,len(a))
    if floor>0:
       a=a+floor #? not needed
    return a/s
```
Don't add floor to all; zeros okay.

`conv_self`:
```
def conv_self(a, mfft=None):
    n=len(a)
    m=2*n-1
    if mfft is None: mfft=pow2_ge(m)
    Fa=np.fft.rfft(a,mfft)
    c=np.fft.irfft(Fa*Fa,mfft)
    return c[:m]
```
`score_seq`:
```
def score_seq(a, mfft=None):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n=len(a)
    sa=float(a.sum())
    if sa < 1e-12: return np.inf
    c=conv_self(a,mfft)
    return float(2.0*n*np.max(c)/(sa*sa))
```
For normalized loops, use `score_norm(a,mfft)` sum=1 maybe but due normalization sum exactly? We can compute same to be safe but faster? Use same; sum cheap.

`grad_from_weights` as above. Need ensure p length L. If n=1, L=1, M=1? For mirror not called n small? Could be parent length small. Implement safe:
```
def grad_from_weights(a,p,n):
    L=2*n-1
    Mcross=3*n-2
    if Mcross <1: Mcross=1
    mfft=pow2_ge(Mcross)
    tmp=np.zeros(mfft)
    tmp[:n]=a
    tmp2=np.zeros(mfft)
    tmp2[:L]=p[::-1]
    A=np.fft.rfft(tmp); P=np.fft.rfft(tmp2)
    C=np.fft.irfft(A*P,mfft)
    # C indices n-1 to 2n-2
    sl=C[n-1:2*n-1]
    return 2.0*sl[::-1]
```
For n=1, N-1=0, 2n-1=1, slice C[0:1], okay. Mcross=1. Good.

`make_target_c(c,Ht,n,blend)`:

```

    L=2*n-1
    if n<=2:
       return np.ones(L)/L
    x=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,L)
    Hc = Ht * float(n)

    if Hc < 0.5: Hc=0.5

    if e < 0: e=0.0
    if e > 2: e=2.0
    r = int(round(e*(n-1)))

`fit_target_sum_cap`:
```
def fit_target_sum_cap(t, Ht):
    t=np.asarray(t,dtype=float).copy()
    if t.size==0: return None
    s=float(t.sum())
    if s <= 0: return None
    # If sum slightly >1, scale down? but that lowers max. To make sum1, if s>1+1e-12: t/=s; if max > Ht maybe cap and repeat.
    for _ in range(5):
       s=float(t.sum())
       if s > 1.0 + 1e-12:
           scale=1.0/s
           t=t*scale
           # scaling may make max <Ht; sum1 done
           continue
       if abs(s-1.0) <= 1e-12:
           t=np.minimum(t,Ht)
           s2=t.sum()
           if s2 <= 0: return None
           if abs(s2-1)<=1e-10: return t
           # if cap reduced sum, add
           deficit=1.0-s2
       else:
           deficit=1.0-s
       if deficit <= 0:
           t=t/t.sum() if t.sum()>0 else None
           return t
       mask=t < Ht - 1e-15
       if not np.any(mask): return None
       room=Ht - t[mask]
       if np.any(room <=0): room=np.maximum(room,1e-300)
       room_sum=float(room.sum())
       if room_sum <= 1e-15: return None
       step=deficit/room_sum
       if step > 1.0:
           # infeasible
           return None
       t[mask] += step*room
    return t
```

```
def fit_target(t,Ht):
    t=np.asarray(t,float)
    t=np.where(t>0,t,0)
    s=float(t.sum())
    if s <=1e-15: return None
    for it in range(10):
        s=float(t.sum())
        if s > 1.0:
            t=t/s
            s=1.0
        if s < 1.0 - 1e-12:
            deficit=1.0-s
            mask=t < Ht - 1e-15
            if not np.any(mask): return None
            room=Ht - t[mask]
            room_sum=float(room.sum())
            if room_sum <= 1e-15: return None
            if deficit > room_sum + 1e-12: return None
            t[mask] += (deficit/room_sum)*room
            continue
        # sum approx 1
        m=max(t)
        if m > Ht*(1+1e-12):
            # reduce values above Ht proportionally? cap and add deficit
            t=np.minimum(t,Ht)
            continue
        return t
    return t
```

Target Newton candidate:
```
def target_step(a,target,mfft,theta):
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,mfft)
    A2=A*A
    Th=np.fft.rfft(target,mfft)
    err=Th-A2
    err[0]=0.0  # preserve sum
    absA=np.abs(A)
    floor=1e-12*float(np.max(absA)) if absA.size else 0
    d=np.zeros_like(A)
    mask=absA>floor
    d[mask]=err[mask]/(2.0*A[mask])
    Anew=A+theta*d
    anew=np.fft.ifft(Anew).real[:len(a)]
    anew=np.maximum(anew,0.0)
    s=anew.sum()
    if s<=1e-12: return None
    return anew/s
```

def score_norm(a,mfft=None):
    n=len(a); sa=float(np.sum(a))
    if sa <=1e-15: return np.inf
    c=conv_self(a,mfft)
    return float(2.0*n*np.max(c)/(sa*sa))
```

`target_newton` details code:
```
def target_newton(a, deadline, blend=0.35, floor_score=1.50, initial_drop=0.004, max_stall=500, symmetric=False):
    a=normalize_seq(a)
    if symmetric: a=sym_seq(a)
    n=len(a); L=2*n-1; mfft=pow2_ge(2*n-1)
    best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=score_norm(a,mfft)
    H_floor=floor_score/(2.0*n)
    min_floor=1.0/(2.0*n-1.0)
    if H_floor < min_floor: H_floor=min_floor
    Ht = max(H_floor, best_sc/(2.0*n) * (1-initial_drop))
    drop=initial_drop
    cur_blend=blend
    no_imp=0
    thetas=(0.05,0.1,0.2,0.4,0.8,1.2)
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       c=conv_self(a,mfft)
       H=float(np.max(c))
       sc=score_norm(a,mfft) # or 2nH since sum1; but sum? use 2nH
       # Since normalized, sum may drift due sym; use sum
       if not finite: break
       if sc < best_sc*(1-1e-14): best_sc=sc; best_a=a.copy()
       if H <= H_floor*(1+1e-9): break
       if Ht >= H: Ht = max(H_floor, H*(1-drop))
       target=make_target_c(c,Ht,n,cur_blend,symmetric)
       if target is None:
          drop=max(0.0005, drop*0.5)
          Ht=max(H_floor, H*(1-drop))
          no_imp += 1
          if no_imp > max(50, max_stall//3): break
          continue
       A=np.fft.rfft(a,mfft)
       Th=np.fft.rfft(target,mfft)
       err=Th - A*A
       # ensure zero freq exact
       if err.size: err=np.asarray(err); err[0]=0.0
       absA=np.abs(A); amin=float(np.max(absA)) if absA.size else 1.0
       floorA=1e-10*amin
       d=np.zeros_like(A, dtype=np.complex128)
       mask=absA>floorA
       np.divide(err, 2.0*A, out=d, where=mask)
       step=np.fft.ifft(d).real[:n]
       if not np.all(np.isfinite(step)): continue
       step = step - float(np.mean(step))
       stmax=float(np.max(np.abs(step)))
       a_max=float(np.max(a))
       if stmax > 0 and a_max > 0:
           cap=0.5*a_max
           if stmax > cap: step *= cap/stmax
       best_cand=None; best_cand_sc=sc
       for th in thetas:
           cand=a + th*step
           if symmetric:
               cand=0.5*(cand+cand[::-1])
           cand=np.maximum(cand,0.0)
           s=float(np.sum(cand))
           if s <= 1e-12: continue
           cand=cand/s
           sc_c=score_norm(cand,mfft)
           if np.isfinite(sc_c) and sc_c < best_cand_sc:
               best_cand_sc=sc_c; best_cand=cand
       if best_cand is not None:
           a=best_cand
           if best_cand_sc < best_sc:
               best_sc=best_cand_sc; best_a=a.copy()
               no_imp=0
               # lower target? keep drop maybe
               if Ht > H_floor:
                   drop = max(0.0008, drop*0.85)
                   Ht = max(H_floor, H*(1-drop))? H outdated. Use new H?
                   # Better after loop next iteration recompute if Ht>=H.
           else:
               no_imp +=1
       else:
           no_imp +=1
       if no_imp > 50:
           drop=max(0.0005, drop*0.6)
           cur_blend = min(0.6, cur_blend+0.05)
           # small perturbation
           a = best_a * (1.0 + 0.005*np.random? no rng passed)
```
We didn't pass rng. Could use deterministic small perturbation based on numpy default? Better avoid randomness in target; use smooth sinusoidal. Or pass rng? `target_newton` can accept rng optional. If we keep Ht, next iteration H maybe >Ht, target still ambitious. Good. If H <=Ht (target achieved), Ht>=H so next set Ht=H*(1-drop) lowers. So no need adjust. Drop maybe slowly reduce to make easier? For ambitious target that is feasible but Newton small, maybe keep. If no_imp, reduce drop (make target easier) and blend more.

But if initial drop 0.004 and no improve, drop reduces; target gets closer to current, may improve. Good.

   while time < deadline:

      p=0.85*p + 0.15/L? Use p = p + 0.05? Let's set `p = 0.90*p + 0.10*(1.0/L)`.

`perturb_seq`:
```
def perturb_seq(a,rng,strength=0.03):
    n=len(a); a=normalize_seq(a)
    x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    p=a.copy()
    p[0:n]=p*(1+strength*rng.standard_normal(n))
    f=1+8*rng.random(); ph=2*np.pi*rng.random(); p=p*(1+0.5*strength*np.cos(2*np.pi*f*x+ph))
    p=np.maximum(p,0); s=p.sum(); if s<=0 return normalize(a); return p/s
```
If no rng, create? We'll ensure rng.

    if np.isfinite(score) and score < best_score:
       print(...)
       return score, np.clip(a,0,1000).copy()
    return best_score,best_a
```

def record(score,a,best_score,best_a,label=''):
    if np.isfinite(score) and score < best_score - 1e-12:
       print(f"  {label}: {score:.8f} n={len(a)}")
       return score, a.copy()

Main code detailed:
```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    t0=time.monotonic()
    budget=float(budget_s)
    eff=budget-45
    if eff<60: eff=max(30,budget-10)
    deadline=t0+eff
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    best_score=np.inf; best_a=np.ones(8192)/8192
    parent=None
    pt=globals().get('height_sequence_1')
    if pt is not None:

`pow2_ge`:
```
def pow2_ge(m):
    m = int(m)
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()
```

`normalize_seq`:
```
def normalize_seq(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1)
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15:
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / a.size
    return a / s
```
`sym_seq`: `return normalize_seq(0.5*(a+a[::-1]))`

`interp_length`:
```
def interp_length(a,n):
    a=normalize_seq(a)
    if len(a)==n: return a
    if n<=0: return np.ones(1)
    if len(a)==1: return np.full(n,a[0])/n
    x_old=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,len(a))
    x_new=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    y=np.interp(x_new,x_old,a)
    return normalize_seq(y)
```

`build_initial_candidates` maybe not needed. But can use in main for fallbacks.

```
def build_fallback_candidates(n,rng,parent):
    cands=[]
    if parent is not None:
       cands.append(interp_length(parent,n))
       cands.append(sym_seq(interp_length(parent,n)))
    x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    cands.append(np.ones(n)/n)
    cands.append(normalize_seq(1.0+0.3*np.cos(np.pi*x)))
    for a in (0.3,0.7,1.2):
       cands.append(normalize_seq(np.power(x+1e-6,a)))
       cands.append(normalize_seq(np.power(1.0-x+1e-6,a)))
    cands.append(normalize_seq(np.clip(1.0+0.1*rng.standard_normal(n),0.01,10.0)))
    return cands
```
No lambda. Use in main to pick best init if parent not.

`fit_target(t,Ht)`: Let's implement robust.
```
def fit_target(t,Ht):
    t=np.asarray(t,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if t.size==0: return None
    t=np.where(t>0.0,t,0.0)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(t)): t=np.nan_to_num(t,nan=0.0,posinf=1000.0,neginf=0.0)
    for _ in range(20):
        s=float(t.sum())
        if s <= 1e-15: return None
        if s > 1.0 + 1e-12:
            t = t / s
            # if max still > Ht? scaling by <1 reduces max, okay
            continue
        if s < 1.0 - 1e-12:
            deficit = 1.0 - s
            mask = t < Ht - 1e-15
            if not np.any(mask): return None
            room = Ht - t[mask]
            room_sum = float(room.sum())
            if room_sum <= 1e-15: return None
            if deficit > room_sum + 1e-10:
                return None
            t[mask] += (deficit / room_sum) * room
            continue
        # sum approx 1
        if float(np.max(t)) > Ht * (1.0 + 1e-10):
            t = np.minimum(t, Ht)
            continue
        return t
    return t
```
If after max cap sum<1, next loop adds. Good.
But if `s > 1` scaling down may make max <Ht, sum1, returns. But target max then lower than Ht; okay.

`make_target_c`:
```
def make_target_c(c,Ht,n,blend=0.0,sym_target=False):
    c=np.asarray(c,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    L=c.size
    t=np.minimum(c,Ht)
    t=np.where(t>0,t,0)
    if sym_target:
       t=0.5*(t+t[::-1])
    if blend>0:
       base=base_target_shape(n,Ht)
       if sym_target: base=0.5*(base+base[::-1])
       t=(1-blend)*t + blend*base
       t=np.where(t>0,t,0)
    return fit_target(t,Ht)
```
Potential issue: If Ht is less than current H and t cap at Ht, then blend with base (base normalized but may have max >Ht) then fit caps. Good.

`target_newton` more precise:
```
def target_newton(a, deadline, blend=0.35, floor_score=1.50, initial_drop=0.004, max_stall=800, symmetric=False, rng=None):
    if rng is None: rng=np.random.default_rng(0)
    a=normalize_seq(a)
    if symmetric: a=sym_seq(a)
    n=len(a); L=2*n-1; mfft=pow2_ge(L)
    best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=score_norm(a,mfft)
    min_H=1.0/float(L)
    H_floor=max(min_H, floor_score/(2.0*n))
    H=best_sc*float(a.sum())/ (2.0*n)? Since a normalized sum1. But to be safe H = score_norm? score=2nH/S², S=1. best_sc/(2n).
    Ht=max(H_floor, best_sc/(2.0*n)*(1.0-initial_drop))
    drop=initial_drop
    cur_blend=blend
    noimp=0
    thetas=(0.05,0.1,0.2,0.4,0.8,1.2)
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       c=conv_self(a,mfft)
       S=float(a.sum())
       H=float(np.max(c))
       sc=2.0*float(n)*H/(S*S)
       if not np.isfinite(sc): break
       if sc < best_sc:
           best_sc=sc; best_a=a.copy()
       if H <= H_floor*(1.0+1e-10): break
       if Ht >= H:
           Ht=max(H_floor, H*(1.0-drop))
       target=make_target_c(c,Ht,n,cur_blend,symmetric)
       if target is None:
           drop=max(0.0005, drop*0.5)
           Ht=max(H_floor, H*(1.0-drop))
           noimp += 1
           if noimp > max(50, max_stall//4): break
           continue
       A=np.fft.rfft(a,mfft)
       Th=np.fft.rfft(target,mfft)
       err=Th - A*A
       if err.size>0:
           err=np.asarray(err)
           err[0]=0.0
       absA=np.abs(A)
       amax=float(np.max(absA)) if absA.size else 1.0
       floorA=1e-10*amax
       d=np.zeros(A.shape, dtype=np.complex128)
       mask=absA>floorA
       try:
          d[mask]=err[mask]/(2.0*A[mask])
       except Exception:
          continue
       step=np.fft.ifft(d).real[:n]
       if not np.all(np.isfinite(step)): continue
       step-=float(step.mean())
       stmax=float(np.max(np.abs(step)))
       amax_a=float(np.max(a))
       if stmax>0 and amax_a>0:
          cap=0.7*amax_a
          if stmax>cap: step=step*(cap/stmax)
       best_cand=None; best_cand_sc=sc
       for th in thetas:
          cand=a + th*step
          if symmetric:
             cand=0.5*(cand+cand[::-1])
          cand=np.maximum(cand,0.0)
          s=float(cand.sum())
          if s <= 1e-12: continue
          cand=cand/s
          sc_c=score_norm(cand,mfft)
          if np.isfinite(sc_c) and sc_c < best_cand_sc:
              best_cand_sc=sc_c; best_cand=cand
       if best_cand is not None:
          a=best_cand
          if best_cand_sc < best_sc:
              best_sc=best_cand_sc; best_a=a.copy(); noimp=0
              cur_blend=max(0.08, cur_blend*0.95)
          else:
              noimp += 1
       else:
          noimp += 1
       if noimp > 40:
          drop=max(0.0004, drop*0.6)
          Ht=max(H_floor, H*(1.0-drop))
          cur_blend=min(0.6, cur_blend+0.06)
          noimp=0
       if noimp > max_stall: break
    return best_sc,best_a
```
The noimp logic reset after 40 but max_stall never increases? Actually after reset, max_stall not reached. Use a separate `stall_count` not reset? We want break after long overall stall. `noimp` reset means max_stall not useful for long. Use `total_stall` increment; if total_stall>max_stall break; `noimp` for short adjust. Modify:

`lse_score_grad`: We'll implement correctly.
```
def lse_score_grad(a,beta):
    a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel(); n=len(a); S=float(a.sum())
    if S<1e-12: return 1e10,np.zeros(n)
    c=conv_self(a)
    cmax=float(np.max(c))
    scaled=beta*(c-cmax)
    exp_scaled=np.exp(scaled)
    exp_sum=float(exp_scaled.sum())
    if exp_sum<=0: return 1e10, zeros
    L=cmax + np.log(exp_sum)/beta
    score=2.0*n*L/(S*S)
    p=exp_scaled/exp_sum
    grad_c=grad_from_weights(a,p,n)
    S2=S*S; S3=S2*S
    grad=(2.0*n)*(grad_c/S2 - 2.0*L/S3)
    return float(score), grad
```
Need `grad_from_weights` uses separate FFT size 3n; okay. In L-BFGS, repeated.

`lse_objective(x,beta): return lse_score_grad(x,beta)`.

`lbfgs_polish`:
```
def lbfgs_polish(a, deadline, betas=(30,100,400)):
    a=normalize_seq(a); n=len(a); best_a=a; best_sc=score_seq(a)
    bounds=[(0.0,1000.0)]*n
    for beta in betas:
       if time.monotonic() >= deadline: break
       if n > 20000: break
       maxfun=1500 if n<8192 else 700
       try:
         res=minimize(lse_objective, a.copy(), args=(beta,), method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds, options={'ftol':1e-15,'gtol':1e-12,'maxiter':800,'maxfun':maxfun,'maxls':30})
         cand=normalize_seq(res.x)
         sc=score_seq(cand)
         if sc<best_sc: best_a=cand; best_sc=sc
         a=best_a.copy()
       except Exception: pass
    return best_sc,best_a
```
Potential issue: L-BFGS may change length? no.
`bounds` list large; for n 16384 okay maybe memory 16k* tuple overhead ~0.8MB. For n=20000 similar. Time.

       best_score,best_a=record(sc1,a1,best_score,best_a,'target main')
       # quick sym
       asy=sym_seq(best_a); scsy=score_seq(asy); update

    # Phase 2 mirror
    dead2 = t0 + 0.55*eff
    if time.monotonic() < dead2:
       sc2,a2=mirror_flatten(best_a, dead2, t_schedule=(10,25,70,200), max_stall=1500, symmetric=False, rng=rng)
       update

    # Phase 3 symmetric target
    dead3 = t0 + 0.68*eff
    if time.monotonic() < dead3:
       a_sym=sym_seq(best_a)
       sc3,a3=target_newton(a_sym, dead3, blend=0.45, floor_score=1.50, initial_drop=0.003, max_stall=1000, symmetric=True, rng=rng)
       update
       a_sym2=sym_seq(best_a); update

    # Phase 4 long length (16384 if not already)
    long_len=16384
    dead4 = t0 + 0.84*eff
    if time.monotonic() < dead4 and len(best_a) != long_len:
       a_long=interp_length(best_a, long_len)
       sc4,a4=target_newton(a_long, dead4, blend=0.35, floor_score=1.50, initial_drop=0.004, max_stall=800, symmetric=False, rng=rng)
       update
       # mirror on best if time
       dead4b = deadline - 0.08*eff
       if time.monotonic() < dead4b:
          sc5,a5=mirror_flatten(best_a, dead4b, t_schedule=(10,30,100), max_stall=800, symmetric=False, rng=rng)
          update
    else:
       # if already long or no time, mirror remaining
       dead4b=deadline - 0.08*eff
       if time.monotonic() < dead4b:
          sc5,a5=mirror_flatten(best_a, dead4b, ...)
          update

    # Phase 5 final target tiny? maybe not
    # Phase 6 LBFGS
    dead6=deadline-30
    if time.monotonic() < dead6 and best_score > 1.0:
       sc6,a6=lbfgs_polish(best_a, dead6, betas=(50,200))
       update

    # final sym
    af=sym_seq(best_a); scf=score_seq(af); update
    # Output
    out_a=best_a
    out=[float(max(0.0,min(1000.0,x))) for x in out_a]
    if not out or sum(out) < 0.01:
       out=[0.5]*64
    if time.monotonic() < t0+budget-5:
       try:
          verified=float(evaluate_sequence(out))
          print(final)
       except Exception as e:
          print('verify failed',e)
    return out
```

```python
"""
Optimizes non-negative step-function heights for the score
    F(a) = 2*n*max(conv(a,a)) / sum(a)^2

The code uses a combination of:
1. Target-Newton iterations: adaptively construct a lower-peak convolution
   target (water-filled plus a smooth base profile) and take damped Newton
   steps in the frequency domain to reduce max convolution.
2. Mirror-descent / multiplicative peak-flattening: exponentiated coordinates
   driven by active-peak subgradients.
3. L-BFGS polishing on a log-sum-exp smoothed proxy, when remaining time allows.

All major routines are top-level, deterministic unless an RNG is supplied, and
respect a time deadline.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize


def pow2_ge(m):
    m = int(m)
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def normalize_seq(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15:
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(a.size)
    return a / s


def sym_seq(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    return normalize_seq(0.5 * (a + a[::-1]))


def interp_length(a, n):
    a = normalize_seq(a)
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    if len(a) == n:
        return a.copy()
    if len(a) == 1:
        return np.full(n, float(a[0]), dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, len(a))
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    y = np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)
    return normalize_seq(y)


def build_fallback_candidates(n, rng, parent):
    cands = []
    n = int(n)
    if parent is not None:
        cands.append(interp_length(parent, n))
        cands.append(sym_seq(interp_length(parent, n)))
    if n > 1:
        x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
        cands.append(normalize_seq(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)))
        cands.append(normalize_seq(1.0 + 0.35 * np.cos(np.pi * x)))
        cands.append(normalize_seq(1.0 - 0.35 * np.cos(np.pi * x)))
        for p in (0.25, 0.5, 0.8, 1.2):
            cands.append(normalize_seq(np.power(x + 1e-7, p)))
            cands.append(normalize_seq(np.power(1.0 - x + 1e-7, p)))
        cands.append(normalize_seq(np.power(x + 1e-7, 0.3) * np.power(1.0 - x + 1e-7, 0.7)))
        cands.append(normalize_seq(np.power(x + 1e-7, 0.7) * np.power(1.0 - x + 1e-7, 0.3)))
    cands.append(normalize_seq(np.clip(1.0 + 0.08 * rng.standard_normal(n), 0.01, 10.0)))
    cands.append(normalize_seq(np.clip(1.0 + 0.25 * rng.standard_normal(n), 0.01, 10.0)))
    return cands


def conv_self(a, mfft=None):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    m = 2 * n - 1
    if mfft is None:
        mfft = pow2_ge(m)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, mfft)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, mfft)
    return c[:m]


def score_seq(a, mfft=None):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 1e-12:
        return float("inf")
    c = conv_self(a, mfft)
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(m) or m < 0.0:
        m = max(0.0, m)
    return float(2.0 * n * m / (s * s))


def score_norm(a, mfft=None):
    # Same as score_seq, but avoids recomputing sum when normalized writes are used.
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 1e-12:
        return float("inf")
    c = conv_self(a, mfft)
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(m) or m < 0.0:
        m = max(0.0, m)
    return float(2.0 * len(a) * m / (s * s))


def grad_from_weights(a, w, n):
    # Gradient of ∑_k w_k (a*a)_k wrt a_i:
    # G_i = 2 * sum_{j=0}^{n-1} w_{i+j} a_j
    # Computed by convolving reversed a with w.
    n = int(n)
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    L = 2 * n - 1
    m = 3 * n - 2
    if m < 1:
        m = 1
    mfft = pow2_ge(m)

    ra = np.zeros(mfft, dtype=np.float64)
    ra[:n] = a[::-1]
    pw = np.zeros(mfft, dtype=np.float64)
    pw[:L] = w
    coeff = np.fft.irfft(np.fft.rfft(ra, mfft) * np.fft.rfft(pw, mfft), mfft)
    return 2.0 * coeff[n - 1:2 * n - 1]


def base_target_shape(n, ht):
    # Desired profile for convolution coefficients:
    # symmetric, endpoints zero, central plateau, max controlled by ht.
    n = int(n)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    if n <= 1:
        return np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64) / float(L)
    hc = float(ht) * float(n)
    if not np.isfinite(hc) or hc < 0.5:
        hc = 0.5
    e = 2.0 - 1.0 / hc
    if e < 0.0:
        e = 0.0
    if e > 2.0:
        e = 2.0
    r = int(round(e * (n - 1)))
    if r < 1:
        r = 1
    if r > L - 1:
        r = L - 1
    k = np.arange(L, dtype=np.float64)
    shape = np.minimum(1.0, np.minimum(k / float(r), (float(L - 1) - k) / float(r)))
    shape = np.maximum(shape, 0.0)
    s = float(shape.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        shape = np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(L)
    return shape / s


def fit_target(t, ht):
    # Make target sum 1, nonnegative, elementwise <= ht if possible.
    t = np.asarray(t, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if t.size == 0:
        return None
    t = np.nan_to_num(t, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    t = np.maximum(t, 0.0)
    if not np.isfinite(ht) or ht <= 0.0:
        return None
    for _ in range(30):
        s = float(t.sum())
        if s <= 1e-15:
            return None
        if s > 1.0 + 1e-12:
            t = t / s
            # scaling down cannot violate cap
            continue
        if s < 1.0 - 1e-12:
            deficit = 1.0 - s
            mask = t < ht - 1e-15
            if not np.any(mask):
                return None
            room = ht - t[mask]
            room_sum = float(room.sum())
            if room_sum <= 1e-15:
                return None
            if deficit > room_sum + 1e-10:
                return None
            t[mask] += (deficit / room_sum) * room
            continue
        if float(np.max(t)) > ht * (1.0 + 1e-10):
            t = np.minimum(t, ht)
            continue
        return t
    return t


def make_target_c(c, ht, n, blend=0.0, sym_target=False):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    t = np.minimum(c, ht)
    t = np.maximum(t, 0.0)
    if sym_target:
        t = 0.5 * (t + t[::-1])
    if blend > 0.0:
        base = base_target_shape(n, ht)
        if sym_target:
            base = 0.5 * (base + base[::-1])
        blend = min(0.99, max(0.0, float(blend)))
        t = (1.0 - blend) * t + blend * base
        t = np.maximum(t, 0.0)
    return fit_target(t, ht)


def perturb_seq(a, rng, strength=0.03):
    a = normalize_seq(a)
    if rng is None:
        rng = np.random.default_rng()
    n = a.size
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    p = a * (1.0 + float(strength) * rng.standard_normal(n))
    freq = 1.0 + 10.0 * rng.random()
    phase = 2.0 * np.pi * rng.random()
    p = p * (1.0 + 0.5 * float(strength) * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * freq * x + phase))
    p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12:
        return a.copy()
    return p / s


def target_newton(a, deadline, blend=0.35, floor_score=1.50,
                  initial_drop=0.004, max_stall=1200,
                  symmetric=False, rng=None):
    """
    Target-Newton peak reduction.
    Builds a lower-peak target close to the current convolution and performs
    damped Newton steps for A^2 ~ target in Fourier space.
    """
    if rng is None:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(0)

    a = normalize_seq(a)
    if symmetric:
        a = sym_seq(a)
    n = int(a.size)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    mfft = pow2_ge(L)

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = score_norm(a, mfft)
    last_sc = best_sc

    min_H = 1.0 / float(L)
    H_floor = max(min_H, float(floor_score) / (2.0 * n))
    H = best_sc / (2.0 * n)
    drop = float(initial_drop)
    Ht = max(H_floor, H * (1.0 - drop))
    cur_blend = float(blend)
    stall_total = 0

    thetas = (0.05, 0.10, 0.20, 0.40, 0.80, 1.20)

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        c = conv_self(a, mfft)
        S = float(a.sum())
        H = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
        sc = float(2.0 * n * H / (S * S)) if S > 0 else float("inf")
        if not np.isfinite(sc):
            break

        if sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = a.copy()

        if H <= H_floor * (1.0 + 1e-10):
            break

        if Ht >= H:
            Ht = max(H_floor, H * (1.0 - drop))

        target = make_target_c(c, Ht, n, cur_blend, symmetric)
        if target is None:
            drop = max(0.0005, drop * 0.5)
            Ht = max(H_floor, H * (1.0 - drop))
            stall_total += 1
            if stall_total > max_stall:
                break
            continue

        # Newton step for A^2 = target
        A = np.fft.rfft(a, mfft)
        At = np.fft.rfft(target, mfft)
        err = At - A * A
        if err.size:
            err = np.asarray(err, dtype=np.complex128)
            err[0] = 0.0

        absA = np.abs(A)
        amax = float(np.max(absA)) if absA.size else 1.0
        if amax <= 0.0:
            continue
        floorA = 1e-10 * amax
        d = np.zeros(A.shape, dtype=np.complex128)
        mask = absA > floorA
        try:
            d[mask] = err[mask] / (2.0 * A[mask])
        except Exception:
            continue

        step = np.fft.ifft(d).real[:n]
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(step)):
            continue
        step = step - float(step.mean())

        stmax = float(np.max(np.abs(step)))
        amax_a = float(np.max(a))
        if stmax > 0.0 and amax_a > 0.0:
            cap = 0.7 * amax_a
            if stmax > cap:
                step = step * (cap / stmax)

        best_cand = None
        best_cand_sc = sc

        for th in thetas:
            cand = a + th * step
            if symmetric:
                cand = 0.5 * (cand + cand[::-1])
            cand = np.maximum(cand, 0.0)
            ss = float(cand.sum())
            if ss <= 1e-12:
                continue
            cand = cand / ss
            sc_c = score_norm(cand, mfft)
            if np.isfinite(sc_c) and sc_c < best_cand_sc:
                best_cand_sc = sc_c
                best_cand = cand

        if best_cand is not None and best_cand_sc < last_sc * (1.0 - 1e-14):
            a = best_cand
            last_sc = best_cand_sc
            stall_total = 0
            if best_cand_sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = best_cand_sc
                best_a = a.copy()
            cur_blend = max(0.08, cur_blend * 0.96)
        else:
            stall_total += 1
            if stall_total > 35:
                drop = max(0.0004, drop * 0.6)
                Ht = max(H_floor, H * (1.0 - drop))
                cur_blend = min(0.65, cur_blend + 0.05)
            if stall_total > 120:
                a = perturb_seq(best_a, rng, 0.01)
                last_sc = score_norm(a, mfft)
                stall_total = 0

        if stall_total > max_stall:
            break

    return best_sc, best_a


def mirror_flatten(a, deadline, t_schedule=None, max_stall=1200,
                   symmetric=False, rng=None):
    """
    Multiplicative/mirror descent using active peak weights.
    The coordinate update is a_i <- a_i * exp(-eta G_i), normalized.
    """
    if rng is None:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(0)
    if t_schedule is None:
        t_schedule = (5.0, 15.0, 40.0, 120.0, 400.0)

    a = normalize_seq(a)
    if symmetric:
        a = sym_seq(a)
    n = int(a.size)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    mfft = pow2_ge(2 * n - 1)

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = score_norm(a, mfft)
    last_sc = best_sc
    ti = 0
    stall_total = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        c = conv_self(a, mfft)
        S = float(a.sum())
        H = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
        sc = float(2.0 * n * H / (S * S)) if S > 0 else float("inf")
        if not np.isfinite(sc):
            break

        if sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = a.copy()

        min_H = 1.0 / float(L)
        if H <= min_H * (1.000001):
            break

        t = float(t_schedule[ti % len(t_schedule)])
        z = t * (c - H) / H if H > 0 else np.zeros_like(c)
        p = np.exp(z)
        ps = float(p.sum())
        if ps <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(ps):
            p = np.full(L, 1.0 / float(L), dtype=np.float64)
        else:
            p = p / ps
        # keep a small full-support component to avoid too sparse updates
        p = 0.90 * p + 0.10 * (1.0 / float(L))

        G = grad_from_weights(a, p, n)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(G)):
            break
        g = G - float(np.mean(G))
        grange = float(np.max(g) - np.min(g))
        if grange <= 1e-18:
            ti += 1
            stall_total += 1
            if stall_total > max_stall:
                break
            continue

        a_pos = np.maximum(a, 1e-300)
        loga = np.log(a_pos)

        best_cand = None
        best_cand_sc = sc

        for kap in (0.02, 0.05, 0.10, 0.20, 0.50, 1.00, 2.00):
            tau = kap / grange
            delta = -tau * g
            dr = float(np.max(delta) - np.min(delta))
            if dr > 40.0:
                delta = delta * (40.0 / dr)
            vals = np.exp(loga + delta - float(np.max(delta)))
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(vals)):
                continue
            ss = float(vals.sum())
            if ss <= 1e-12:
                continue
            cand = vals / ss
            if symmetric:
                cand = 0.5 * (cand + cand[::-1])
            sc_c = score_norm(cand, mfft)
            if np.isfinite(sc_c) and sc_c < best_cand_sc:
                best_cand_sc = sc_c
                best_cand = cand

        if best_cand is not None and best_cand_sc < last_sc * (1.0 - 1e-14):
            a = best_cand
            last_sc = best_cand_sc
            stall_total = 0
            if best_cand_sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = best_cand_sc
                best_a = a.copy()
            ti = (ti + 1) % len(t_schedule)
        else:
            stall_total += 1
            ti = (ti + 1) % len(t_schedule)
            if stall_total > 40:
                # alternate broad/narrow active sets
                ti = (ti + max(1, len(t_schedule) // 2)) % len(t_schedule)
            if stall_total > 140:
                a = perturb_seq(best_a, rng, 0.012)
                last_sc = score_norm(a, mfft)
                stall_total = 0

        if stall_total > max_stall:
            break

    return best_sc, best_a


def lse_score_grad(a, beta):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    S = float(a.sum())
    if S < 1e-12:
        return 1e10, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    c = conv_self(a)
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    scaled = float(beta) * (c - cmax)
    exp_scaled = np.exp(scaled)
    exp_sum = float(exp_scaled.sum())
    if exp_sum <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(exp_sum):
        return 1e10, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    L = cmax + float(np.log(exp_sum)) / float(beta)
    score = float(2.0 * n * L / (S * S))
    p = exp_scaled / exp_sum
    grad_c = grad_from_weights(a, p, n)
    S2 = S * S
    S3 = S2 * S
    grad = (2.0 * n) * (grad_c / S2 - 2.0 * L / S3)
    return score, grad


def lse_objective(x, beta):
    return lse_score_grad(x, beta)


def lbfgs_polish(a, deadline, betas=(50.0, 200.0)):
    """
    Conservative L-BFGS polish on the smoothed LSE objective.
    """
    a = normalize_seq(a)
    n = int(a.size)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = score_seq(a)

    if n > 20000:
        return best_sc, best_a

    try:
        bounds = [(0.0, 1000.0)] * n
    except Exception:
        return best_sc, best_a

    for beta in betas:
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
            break
        if not np.isfinite(best_sc):
            break
        maxfun = 800 if n <= 8192 else 500
        try:
            res = minimize(
                lse_objective,
                a.copy(),
                args=(float(beta),),
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                bounds=bounds,
                jac=True,
                options={
                    "ftol": 1e-15,
                    "gtol": 1e-12,
                    "maxiter": 700,
                    "maxfun": maxfun,
                    "maxls": 30,
                },
            )
            cand = normalize_seq(res.x)
            sc = score_seq(cand)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc
                best_a = cand.copy()
            a = best_a.copy()
        except Exception:
            break
    return best_sc, best_a


def record(score, cand, best_score, best_a, label=""):
    if np.isfinite(score) and score < best_score:
        if label:
            print(f"  {label}: {score:.10f}  n={cand.size if hasattr(cand, 'size') else len(cand)}")
        cand = np.asarray(cand, dtype=np.float64).ravel().copy()
        return float(score), cand
    return best_score, best_a


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    """
    Main search interface. Returns the best list of nonnegative heights found
    within the supplied time budget.
    """
    if budget_s is None:
        budget_s = 2400
    try:
        budget = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget = 2400.0
    if budget < 60.0:
        budget = 60.0

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    reserve = min(60.0, 0.02 * budget + 20.0)
    eff = budget - reserve
    if eff < 60.0:
        eff = budget - 5.0
    deadline = t0 + eff
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    best_score = float("inf")
    best_a = normalize_seq(np.ones(8192, dtype=np.float64))

    parent = None
    stored = globals().get("height_sequence_1")
    if stored is not None:
        try:
            p = np.asarray(stored, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            p = np.nan_to_num(p, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
            p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
            if p.size > 0 and p.sum() > 0.01:
                parent = p.copy()
                sp = score_seq(p)
                best_score, best_a = record(sp, p, best_score, best_a, "stored parent")
        except Exception:
            parent = None

    if parent is None:
        for n0 in (2048, 4096, 8192):
            for cand in build_fallback_candidates(n0, rng, None):
                sc = score_seq(cand)
                best_score, best_a = record(sc, cand, best_score, best_a, "fallback")
    else:
        # Try symmetric version of stored parent.
        try:
            ps = sym_seq(parent)
            sps = score_seq(ps)
            best_score, best_a = record(sps, ps, best_score, best_a, "stored sym")
        except Exception:
            pass
        # Also a few independent fallback starts, cheaply scored.
        for n0 in (4096, 8192):
            for cand in build_fallback_candidates(n0, rng, None):
                sc = score_seq(cand)
                best_score, best_a = record(sc, cand, best_score, best_a, "fallback side")

    # Normalize stored best to unit sum for internal algorithms.
    best_a = normalize_seq(best_a)
    sp = score_seq(best_a)
    if np.isfinite(sp) and sp < best_score:
        best_score = sp

    main_len = len(parent) if parent is not None else 8192
    if main_len < 1024 or main_len > 16384:
        main_len = 8192

    if best_a.size == main_len:
        a0 = normalize_seq(best_a)
    else:
        a0 = interp_length(best_a, main_len)
        sc0 = score_seq(a0)
        best_score, best_a = record(sc0, a0, best_score, best_a, "main interp")
        a0 = best_a.copy()

    # Phase 1: target-newton on primary length.
    dead1 = t0 + 0.32 * eff
    if time.monotonic() < dead1:
        sc1, a1 = target_newton(
            a0, dead1,
            blend=0.35,
            floor_score=1.50,
            initial_drop=0.004,
            max_stall=1500,
            symmetric=False,
            rng=rng,
        )
        best_score, best_a = record(sc1, a1, best_score, best_a, "target main")
        asy = sym_seq(best_a)
        scsy = score_seq(asy)
        best_score, best_a = record(scsy, asy, best_score, best_a, "sym after target")

    # Phase 2: mirror peak flattening on current best.
    dead2 = t0 + 0.55 * eff
    if time.monotonic() < dead2:
        sc2, a2 = mirror_flatten(
            best_a, dead2,
            t_schedule=(10.0, 25.0, 70.0, 200.0),
            max_stall=1500,
            symmetric=False,
            rng=rng,
        )
        best_score, best_a = record(sc2, a2, best_score, best_a, "mirror main")
        asy = sym_seq(best_a)
        scsy = score_seq(asy)
        best_score, best_a = record(scsy, asy, best_score, best_a, "sym after mirror")

    # Phase 3: enforce symmetry in a dedicated target-newton run.
    dead3 = t0 + 0.68 * eff
    if time.monotonic() < dead3:
        a_sym = sym_seq(best_a)
        sc3, a3 = target_newton(
            a_sym, dead3,
            blend=0.45,
            floor_score=1.50,
            initial_drop=0.003,
            max_stall=900,
            symmetric=True,
            rng=rng,
        )
        best_score, best_a = record(sc3, a3, best_score, best_a, "target symmetric")
        asy = sym_seq(best_a)
        scsy = score_seq(asy)
        best_score, best_a = record(scsy, asy, best_score, best_a, "sym final sym-run")

    # Phase 4: attempt longer length (16384) if not already there.
    long_len = 16384
    dead4 = t0 + 0.84 * eff
    dead4b = deadline - 0.08 * eff
    if time.monotonic() < dead4 and best_a.size != long_len:
        a_long = interp_length(best_a, long_len)
        sc4, a4 = target_newton(
            a_long, dead4,
            blend=0.35,
            floor_score=1.50,
            initial_drop=0.004,
            max_stall=900,
            symmetric=False,
            rng=rng,
        )
        best_score, best_a = record(sc4, a4, best_score, best_a, "target long 16k")
        asy = sym_seq(best_a)
        scsy = score_seq(asy)
        best_score, best_a = record(scsy, asy, best_score, best_a, "sym long")

    if time.monotonic() < dead4b:
        sc5, a5 = mirror_flatten(
            best_a, dead4b,
            t_schedule=(10.0, 30.0, 100.0, 300.0),
            max_stall=800,
            symmetric=False,
            rng=rng,
        )
        best_score, best_a = record(sc5, a5, best_score, best_a, "mirror late")
        asy = sym_seq(best_a)
        scsy = score_seq(asy)
        best_score, best_a = record(scsy, asy, best_score, best_a, "sym late")

    # Phase 5: L-BFGS polish, only if enough time and length is manageable.
    dead6 = deadline - 30.0
    if time.monotonic() < dead6 and best_a.size <= 20000:
        sc6, a6 = lbfgs_polish(best_a, dead6, betas=(50.0, 200.0))
        best_score, best_a = record(sc6, a6, best_score, best_a, "lbfgs polish")
        asy = sym_seq(best_a)
        scsy = score_seq(asy)
        best_score, best_a = record(scsy, asy, best_score, best_a, "sym after LBFGS")

    # Final symmetry check.
    asy = sym_seq(best_a)
    scsy = score_seq(asy)
    best_score, best_a = record(scsy, asy, best_score, best_a, "final sym")

    # Prepare safe output.
    out_a = np.asarray(best_a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    out_a = np.nan_to_num(out_a, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    out_a = np.clip(out_a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    out = [float(x) for x in out_a]

    if not out or sum(out) < 0.01:
        out = [0.5] * 64

    # Optional local sanity verification if budget allows.
    if time.monotonic() < t0 + budget - 5.0:
        try:
            verified = float(evaluate_sequence(out))
            print(f"FINAL internal={best_score:.10f} verified={verified:.10f} len={len(out)} "
                  f"time={time.monotonic() - t0:.1f}s")
        except Exception as ee:
            print(f"FINAL internal={best_score:.10f} verify_failed={ee} len={len(out)}")
    else:
        print(f"FINAL internal={best_score:.10f} len={len(out)} (no verify time)")

    return out
```
